    , prgmAnnots =
        [ /Catln/#md
            ( EAppArg
                { eaaArg = Value /text -> /Data/String/Text
                    ( /chars=∀/Data/List/ConsList[ /T=/Data/Char ] ) = "# Strings
                  A [String] is a list of characters: a [String/Text], whose one field [chars] holds them in
                  order. So a string is taken apart and built up the way any list is, character by character.
                  It is a type of its own holding a list rather than the list itself. A list is displayed as its
                  items in brackets and a string as its text, and an empty string is not an empty list of
                  anything else — neither of which could be told apart at runtime were a string only its list.
                  [String] is a class with the one member rather than a data type so that [String] written as a
                  type means every string: a data type's bare name means its argument-less form, which a string
                  with characters is not.
                  Everything a string does is written here over the list of its characters. The operations
                  recur over the characters directly rather than through the list operations of @core/list.ct@,
                  which builds a list's text out of strings and so imports this page."
                }
            )
